# META
~~~ini
description=ref item / ref action keep the authored usage metaclass so inherited type members resolve
specification=OMG SysML 2.0 (formal/26-03-02)
specification_url=https://www.omg.org/spec/SysML/2.0/Beta2/PDF
type=file
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Remaining {
    item def Box {
        item envelopingShapes :>> Items::Item::envelopingShapes {
            ref item envelopedItem :>> that;
            attribute dim = envelopedItem.innerSpaceDimension;
            attribute outer = envelopedItem.outerSpaceDimension;
        }
    }
    flow def Message {
        private ref action thisConnection = self;
        ref action seBeforeNum = thisConnection.start;
        ref action teAfterNum = thisConnection.done;
    }
}
~~~
